# Chapter 1 — Reunion at a familiar house

## Bilingual editing copy

Exported from the current game on 4 October 2026. Includes every branch of the currently playable Chapter 1.

This is an editing copy; changing it does not change the game automatically. Keep scene and line IDs when editing so revisions can be matched reliably. Scene sections follow source order; use the “Next” and choice destinations to follow a particular route. Alternative branches are not all played in a single run.

Narration has no speaker label. MC’s silent thoughts are italicized. Spoken dialogue has a speaker label. Bracketed notes are implementation directions. Placeholders such as {name} remain for player customization. English narration shared by both modes is intentionally repeated. These are the translations currently used by the game, including any existing wording issues or fallback text, rather than a newly rewritten translation.

## 01 / The road back [journey]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[Existing CG: way-back. Begins at the line containing “The sea appears”; ends at the line containing “SAINT LUIS!”.]

| Line / direction | English | Taglish |
|---|---|---|
| journey:0 | It’s been eight hours since we left the city, including the stopover. I can’t get comfortable anymore. | It’s been eight hours since we left the city, including the stopover. I can’t get comfortable anymore. |
| journey:1 | I move my knee away from the seat in front of me. The window keeps rattling beside my head. | I move my knee away from the seat in front of me. The window keeps rattling beside my head. |
| journey:2 | I grew up in Saint Luis with Lola nearby. | I grew up in Saint Luis with Lola nearby. |
| journey:3 | My parents took me to Manila when I was eleven. | My parents took me to Manila when I was eleven. |
| journey:4 | I kept hoping I’d come back the next year. I never did. | I kept hoping I’d come back the next year. I never did. |
| journey:5 | Twelve years have passed. I’m twenty-three now. | Twelve years have passed. I’m twenty-three now. |
| journey:6 | The sea appears between two buildings. I lean closer to the window to get a better look. | The sea appears between two buildings. I lean closer to the window to get a better look. |
| journey:7 | *I’m finally back in Saint Luis.* | *I’m finally back in Saint Luis.* |
| journey:8 | There are small fishing boats in the bay. I can see the old baywalk along the shore. | There are small fishing boats in the bay. I can see the old baywalk along the shore. |
| journey:9 | Lola used to take Rowan and me for walks there in the afternoons. | Lola used to take Rowan and me for walks there in the afternoons. |
| journey:10 | She held our hands when we crossed the road. Me on one side, Rowan on the other. | She held our hands when we crossed the road. Me on one side, Rowan on the other. |
| journey:11 | He’d complain that we weren’t little anymore. Then keep holding on after we’d crossed. | He’d complain that we weren’t little anymore. Then keep holding on after we’d crossed. |
| journey:12 | **MC:** I wonder how he’s been. | **MC:** Hay. I wonder how he’s been. |
| journey:13 | Thinking about him makes me smile. | Thinking about him makes me smile. |
| journey:14 | Then I remember Lola’s funeral. | Then I remember Lola’s funeral. |
| journey:15 | *He was probably at the funeral.* | *He was probably at the funeral.* |
| journey:16 | Two years ago, Lola died during finals week. | Two years ago, Lola died during finals week. |
| journey:17 | My parents said I couldn’t miss my exams. | My parents said I couldn’t miss my exams. |
| journey:18 | I wanted to argue. Instead, I asked if we could go afterward. | I wanted to argue. Instead, I asked if we could go afterward. |
| journey:19 | “We’ll talk about it.” | “We’ll talk about it.” |
| journey:20 | We never did. | We never did. |
| journey:21 | I still took the exams. Handed everything in on time. | I still took the exams. Handed everything in on time. |
| journey:22 | *I was her grandchild, and I wasn’t there.* | *I was her grandchild, and I wasn’t there.* |
| journey:23 | This trip is different. | This trip is different. |
| journey:24 | I paid for the ticket with money I’d saved. | I paid for the ticket with money I’d saved. |
| journey:25 | I told my parents I was leaving. I didn’t ask. | I told my parents I was leaving. I didn’t ask. |
| journey:26 | Then I left before we could finish another argument. | Then I left before we could finish another argument. |
| journey:27 | I needed a break from college. From home. From having the same conversation in my head. | I needed a break from college. From home. From having the same conversation in my head. |
| journey:28 | Lola’s house needs cleaning. A few things probably need fixing. | Lola’s house needs cleaning. A few things probably need fixing. |
| journey:29 | *I can do that.* | *I can do that.* |
| journey:30 | *Sweep a floor. Fix a window. Get some sleep.* | *Sweep a floor. Fix a window. Get some sleep.* |
| journey:31 | *That was the plan.* | *Ok may plan na.* |
| journey:32 | I look back toward the baywalk. | I look back toward the baywalk. |
| journey:33 | *What am I going to say if I see Rowan?* | *What am I going to say if I see Rowan?* |
| journey:34 | *Sorry I stopped calling?* | *Sorry I stopped calling?* |
| journey:35 | *Sorry I believed you’d forgotten me?* | *Sorry I believed you’d forgotten me?* |
| journey:36 | **Conductor:** SAINT LUIS! SAINT LUIS! | **Conductor:** SAINT LUIS! SAINT LUIS! |
| journey:37 | The shout makes me jump. It wakes the passenger across the aisle too. | The shout makes me jump. It wakes the passenger across the aisle too. |
| journey:38 | **MC:** Whoa. | **MC:** Grabe. |
| journey:39 | *At least I’m not the only one he scared.* | *At least I’m not the only one he scared.* |

## A small kindness [driver]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

| Line / direction | English | Taglish |
|---|---|---|
| driver:0 | **MC:** Excuse me, sir? | **MC:** Manong? Excuse me po. |
| driver:1 | The driver looks over. | The driver looks over. |
| driver:2 | **MC:** Will you be passing through Santiago Street? | **MC:** Dadaan po ba kayo sa Santiago Street? |
| driver:3 | **Driver:** Yes. Is that where you’re getting off? | **Driver:** Oo. Doon ka bababa? |
| driver:4 | **MC:** Yes, sir. | **MC:** Opo. |
| driver:5 | I feel a little better. At least the bus goes where I need to go. | I feel a little better. At least the bus goes where I need to go. |
| driver:6 | **MC:** Thank you. | **MC:** Thank you po. |
| driver:7 | Then I notice the tickets in the conductor’s hand. | Then I notice the tickets in the conductor’s hand. |
| driver:8 | *Will I have to pay extra to stay on? I don’t have any cash left.* | *Will I have to pay extra to stay on? I don’t have any cash left.* |
| driver:9 | I briefly think about getting off before they ask me to pay. But then I’d still be stuck here. | I briefly think about getting off before they ask me to pay. But then I’d still be stuck here. |
| driver:10 | **MC:** Is there an extra fare? Because— | **MC:** May dagdag po ba? Kasi— |
| driver:11 | **Driver:** Take a seat. I won’t charge you extra. | **Driver:** Upo ka na. Hindi na kita sisingilin. |
| driver:12 | Before I can explain, he points toward where I fell. | Before I can explain, he points toward where I fell. |
| driver:13 | **Driver:** I saw someone bump into you. Did you lose something? | **Driver:** Nakita kong may nakabangga sayo. May nawala? |
| driver:14 | **MC:** My wallet, sir. | **MC:** Wallet ko po. |
| driver:15 | The driver sighs. | The driver sighs. |
| driver:16 | **Driver:** All right. I’ll drop you off at Santiago. | **Driver:** Hay. Sige. Sa Santiago kita ibababa. |
| driver:17 | **MC:** Thank you. Really. | **MC:** Salamat po. Talaga. |
| driver:18 | He points to an empty seat. | He points to an empty seat. |
| driver:19 | **Driver:** Keep a good hold on your bag. | **Driver:** Hawakang mabuti ’yang bag mo. |
| driver:20 | **MC:** Yes, sir. | **MC:** Opo. |
| driver:21 | I sit down and keep my bag on my lap as the bus starts moving again. | I sit down and keep my bag on my lap as the bus starts moving again. |
| driver:22 | I watch the houses pass by the window. | I watch the houses pass by the window. |
| driver:23 | I’m still upset about my wallet. | I’m still upset about my wallet. |
| driver:24 | But I don’t have to figure out the rest of the trip standing in the aisle. | But I don’t have to figure out the rest of the trip standing in the aisle. |
| driver:25 | *For now, I can sit.* | *For now, I can sit.* |

## He’s here [rGreeting]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day.]

| aTopic1_mayumi_start:6 | **MC:** I can live with that. | **MC:** I can live with that. |

## Dos [aTopic2_school_rival]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

| Line / direction | English | Taglish |
|---|---|---|
| aTopic2_school_rival:0 | **MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival. | **MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:1 — Expression: neutral | **Rowan:** So-called? | **Rowan:** So-called? |
| aTopic2_school_rival:2 | **MC:** Our classmates decided it. Now everything we do gets compared. | **MC:** Classmates namin ang nag-decide. Ngayon lahat na lang kino-compare. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:3 | **MC:** He calls me “Dos.” | **MC:** “Dos” ’yung tawag niya sa ’kin. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:4 | **Rowan:** As in second? | **Rowan:** As in second? |
| aTopic2_school_rival:5 | **MC:** Yeah. | **MC:** Yeah. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:6 — Expression: concerned | Rowan’s smile fades. | Rowan’s smile fades. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:7 | **MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something. | **MC:** Student council president pa siya, so laging may reason para ma-excuse. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:8 | **MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important. | **MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:9 | **MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it. | **MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:10 | **Rowan:** And you end up doing more? | **Rowan:** Tapos ikaw ’yung sumasalo? |
| aTopic2_school_rival:11 | **MC:** Sometimes. Or I’m expected to be fine with it. | **MC:** Minsan. O dapat okay lang sa ’kin. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:12 | **MC:** That’s what gets me. He comes back and acts like nothing happened. | **MC:** ’Yon ’yung nakakainis. Babalik siya na parang walang nangyari. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:13 | **MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke. | **MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:14 | I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down. | I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:15 | **MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs. | **MC:** Isa siya sa dahilan kung bakit hindi na ako sumasali sa orgs. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:16 | **MC:** And part of why I— | **MC:** And part of why I— |
| aTopic2_school_rival:17 | I stop. Rowan waits. | I stop. Rowan waits. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:18 | **MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to unload all that on you. | **MC:** Sorry. Ang dami ko nang nasabi. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:19 | **Rowan:** You don’t have to apologize for telling me. | **Rowan:** Hindi mo kailangang mag-sorry sa pagkukuwento. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:20 | **MC:** Can we leave the rest for another time? | **MC:** Sa ibang araw na lang ’yung iba? |
| aTopic2_school_rival:21 | **Rowan:** Of course. | **Rowan:** Of course. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:22 | He leans back. | He leans back. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:23 | **Rowan:** Do you want me to say something, or would you rather just leave it there? | **Rowan:** Gusto mo bang may sabihin ako, o dito muna tayo? |
| aTopic2_school_rival:24 | **MC:** Leave it there. For now. | **MC:** Dito muna. For now. |
| aTopic2_school_rival:25 | **Rowan:** Okay. | **Rowan:** Okay. |

| aTopic2_rowan_start:17 | **MC:** What do you want to do? | **MC:** Anong gusto mong gawin? |
| aTopic2_rowan_start:18 | **Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it. | **Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it. |
| aTopic2_rowan_start:19 | **MC:** You can tell me when you’re ready. | **MC:** Sabihin mo na lang kapag ready ka na. |
| aTopic2_rowan_start:20 — Expression: smile | **Rowan:** Thanks. | **Rowan:** Thanks. |

| aTopic2_rowan_start:23 | **MC:** What? | **MC:** What? |
| aTopic2_rowan_start:24 | **Rowan:** I still like having you around. | **Rowan:** Gusto pa rin kitang kasama. |

| aTopic4_mayumi_start:0 | **MC:** How’s Tita Mayumi? | **MC:** Kumusta si Tita Mayumi? |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:1 — Expression: neutral | **Rowan:** Busy. Still insists she isn’t. | **Rowan:** Busy. Ayaw pa ring aminin. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:2 | **MC:** That sounds familiar. | **MC:** That sounds familiar. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:3 — Expression: smile | **Rowan:** She’ll be happy you’re back. | **Rowan:** Matutuwa siyang bumalik ka. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:4 | **MC:** I’d like to see her. | **MC:** Gusto ko siyang makita. |

| aTopic4_mayumi_start:6 | **MC:** I can live with that. | **MC:** I can live with that. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:7 | He smiles. | He smiles. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:8 | **MC:** Where’s she working now? | **MC:** Saan siya nagtatrabaho ngayon? |

| aTopic4_mayumi_start:10 | **Rowan:** Then she comes home and somehow still has energy to ask about my day. | **Rowan:** Tapos pag-uwi niya, may energy pa rin siyang mangumusta. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:11 | **MC:** Do you help her out? | **MC:** Tinutulungan mo siya? |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:12 | **Rowan:** I try. Usually she tells me to finish things here first. | **Rowan:** Sinusubukan ko. Kadalasan pinapatapos niya muna ’yung ginagawa ko rito. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:13 | He looks down at his hands. | He looks down at his hands. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:14 — Expression: playful | **Rowan:** Very unfair. All this strength, and she carries the groceries in before I reach the door. | **Rowan:** Unfair nga. Ang lakas-lakas ko, pero naipasok na niya ’yung groceries bago pa ako makarating sa pinto. |
| aTopic4_mayumi_start:15 | **MC:** Maybe she’s faster than you. | **MC:** Baka mas mabilis lang siya sa ’yo. |

| aTopic6_lola_yes:5 | **Rowan:** No. | **Rowan:** No. |

| aTopic6_lola_yes:7 | **Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait. | **Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban. |

| aTopic6_lola_yes:10 — Expression: concerned | **Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip. | **Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe. |

| aTopic6_lola_yes:18 | He looks at me. | He looks at me. |

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

| Line / direction | English | Taglish |
|---|---|---|
| aTopic8_school_rival:0 | **MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival. | **MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:1 — Expression: neutral | **Rowan:** So-called? | **Rowan:** So-called? |
| aTopic8_school_rival:2 | **MC:** Our classmates decided it. Now everything we do gets compared. | **MC:** Classmates namin ang nag-decide. Ngayon lahat na lang kino-compare. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:3 | **MC:** He calls me “Dos.” | **MC:** “Dos” ’yung tawag niya sa ’kin. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:4 | **Rowan:** As in second? | **Rowan:** As in second? |
| aTopic8_school_rival:5 | **MC:** Yeah. | **MC:** Yeah. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:6 — Expression: concerned | Rowan’s smile fades. | Rowan’s smile fades. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:7 | **MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something. | **MC:** Student council president pa siya, so laging may reason para ma-excuse. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:8 | **MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important. | **MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:9 | **MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it. | **MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:10 | **Rowan:** And you end up doing more? | **Rowan:** Tapos ikaw ’yung sumasalo? |
| aTopic8_school_rival:11 | **MC:** Sometimes. Or I’m expected to be fine with it. | **MC:** Minsan. O dapat okay lang sa ’kin. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:12 | **MC:** That’s what gets me. He comes back and acts like nothing happened. | **MC:** ’Yon ’yung nakakainis. Babalik siya na parang walang nangyari. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:13 | **MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke. | **MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:14 | I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down. | I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:15 | **MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs. | **MC:** Isa siya sa dahilan kung bakit hindi na ako sumasali sa orgs. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:16 | **MC:** And part of why I— | **MC:** And part of why I— |
| aTopic8_school_rival:17 | I stop. Rowan waits. | I stop. Rowan waits. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:18 | **MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to unload all that on you. | **MC:** Sorry. Ang dami ko nang nasabi. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:19 | **Rowan:** You don’t have to apologize for telling me. | **Rowan:** Hindi mo kailangang mag-sorry sa pagkukuwento. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:20 | **MC:** Can we leave the rest for another time? | **MC:** Sa ibang araw na lang ’yung iba? |
| aTopic8_school_rival:21 | **Rowan:** Of course. | **Rowan:** Of course. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:22 | He leans back. | He leans back. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:23 | **Rowan:** Do you want me to say something, or would you rather just leave it there? | **Rowan:** Gusto mo bang may sabihin ako, o dito muna tayo? |
| aTopic8_school_rival:24 | **MC:** Leave it there. For now. | **MC:** Dito muna. For now. |
| aTopic8_school_rival:25 | **Rowan:** Okay. | **Rowan:** Okay. |

| aTopic9_mayumi_start:0 | **MC:** How’s Tita Mayumi? | **MC:** Kumusta si Tita Mayumi? |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:1 — Expression: neutral | **Rowan:** Busy. Still insists she isn’t. | **Rowan:** Busy. Ayaw pa ring aminin. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:2 | **MC:** That sounds familiar. | **MC:** That sounds familiar. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:3 — Expression: smile | **Rowan:** She’ll be happy you’re back. | **Rowan:** Matutuwa siyang bumalik ka. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:4 | **MC:** I’d like to see her. | **MC:** Gusto ko siyang makita. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:5 | **Rowan:** I’ll tell her. Then you’ll have to accept whatever food she decides you haven’t been eating enough of. | **Rowan:** Sasabihin ko. Tapos kailangan mong tanggapin lahat ng pagkaing sa tingin niya kulang sa diet mo. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:6 | **MC:** I can live with that. | **MC:** I can live with that. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:7 | He smiles. | He smiles. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:8 | **MC:** Where’s she working now? | **MC:** Saan siya nagtatrabaho ngayon? |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:9 | **Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts. | **Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:10 | **Rowan:** Then she comes home and somehow still has energy to ask about my day. | **Rowan:** Tapos pag-uwi niya, may energy pa rin siyang mangumusta. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:11 | **MC:** Do you help her out? | **MC:** Tinutulungan mo siya? |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:12 | **Rowan:** I try. Usually she tells me to finish things here first. | **Rowan:** Sinusubukan ko. Kadalasan pinapatapos niya muna ’yung ginagawa ko rito. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:13 | He looks down at his hands. | He looks down at his hands. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:14 — Expression: playful | **Rowan:** Very unfair. All this strength, and she carries the groceries in before I reach the door. | **Rowan:** Unfair nga. Ang lakas-lakas ko, pero naipasok na niya ’yung groceries bago pa ako makarating sa pinto. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:15 | **MC:** Maybe she’s faster than you. | **MC:** Baka mas mabilis lang siya sa ’yo. |
| aTopic9_mayumi_start:16 | **Rowan:** She is. Please don’t tell her I admitted it. | **Rowan:** Oo. Huwag mong sabihin na inamin ko. |

| aTopic10_lola_yes:2 | **Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down. | **Rowan:** Mas mabilis na siyang mapagod. Minsan kailangan niyang huminto at umupo kahit hindi pa tapos ’yung ginagawa niya. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:3 | **Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city. | **Rowan:** Nag-aalala ’yung doctor sa puso niya. Gusto niyang magpatingin si Lola sa specialist sa city. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:4 | **MC:** Did she go? | **MC:** Pumunta siya? |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:5 | **Rowan:** No. | **Rowan:** No. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:6 | I wait for him to continue. | I wait for him to continue. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:7 | **Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait. | **Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:8 | **Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too. | **Rowan:** Sasamahan sana siya ni Mama. Ako rin. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:9 | **MC:** What about my parents? | **MC:** Paano ’yung parents ko? |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:10 — Expression: concerned | **Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip. | **Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:11 | **MC:** You heard her? | **MC:** You heard her? |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:12 | **Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side. | **Rowan:** Nando’n ako noong tumawag siya. Pero side niya lang ’yung narinig ko. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:13 | He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers. | He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:14 | **Rowan:** Afterward, she said she didn’t want to ask again. | **Rowan:** After no’n, ayaw na raw niyang magtanong ulit. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:15 | **MC:** What did they say to her? | **MC:** Anong sinabi nila sa kanya? |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:16 | **Rowan:** I don’t know. | **Rowan:** I don’t know. |
| aTopic10_lola_yes:17 | **MC:** They told me she was fine. | **MC:** Sabi nila okay lang siya. |

| aTopic10_school_rival:0 | **MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival. | **MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival. |

| aTopic10_school_rival:5 | **MC:** Yeah. | **MC:** Yeah. |
| aTopic10_school_rival:6 — Expression: concerned | Rowan’s smile fades. | Rowan’s smile fades. |
| aTopic10_school_rival:7 | **MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something. | **MC:** Student council president pa siya, so laging may reason para ma-excuse. |

| aTopic10_school_rival:15 | **MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs. | **MC:** Isa siya sa dahilan kung bakit hindi na ako sumasali sa orgs. |
| aTopic10_school_rival:16 | **MC:** And part of why I— | **MC:** And part of why I— |

## What happened [aTopic12_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

| Line / direction | English | Taglish |
|---|---|---|
| aTopic12_lola_yes:0 | **MC:** Yes. Please. | **MC:** Yes. Please. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:1 — Expression: sad | Rowan looks down at his hands. | Rowan looks down at his hands. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:2 | **Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down. | **Rowan:** Mas mabilis na siyang mapagod. Minsan kailangan niyang huminto at umupo kahit hindi pa tapos ’yung ginagawa niya. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:3 | **Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city. | **Rowan:** Nag-aalala ’yung doctor sa puso niya. Gusto niyang magpatingin si Lola sa specialist sa city. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:4 | **MC:** Did she go? | **MC:** Pumunta siya? |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:5 | **Rowan:** No. | **Rowan:** No. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:6 | I wait for him to continue. | I wait for him to continue. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:7 | **Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait. | **Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:8 | **Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too. | **Rowan:** Sasamahan sana siya ni Mama. Ako rin. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:9 | **MC:** What about my parents? | **MC:** Paano ’yung parents ko? |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:10 — Expression: concerned | **Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip. | **Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe. |
| aTopic12_lola_yes:11 | **MC:** You heard her? | **MC:** You heard her? |

## Tita Mayumi [aTopic12_mayumi_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

| Line / direction | English | Taglish |
|---|---|---|
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:0 | **MC:** How’s Tita Mayumi? | **MC:** Kumusta si Tita Mayumi? |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:1 — Expression: neutral | **Rowan:** Busy. Still insists she isn’t. | **Rowan:** Busy. Ayaw pa ring aminin. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:2 | **MC:** That sounds familiar. | **MC:** That sounds familiar. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:3 — Expression: smile | **Rowan:** She’ll be happy you’re back. | **Rowan:** Matutuwa siyang bumalik ka. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:4 | **MC:** I’d like to see her. | **MC:** Gusto ko siyang makita. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:5 | **Rowan:** I’ll tell her. Then you’ll have to accept whatever food she decides you haven’t been eating enough of. | **Rowan:** Sasabihin ko. Tapos kailangan mong tanggapin lahat ng pagkaing sa tingin niya kulang sa diet mo. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:6 | **MC:** I can live with that. | **MC:** I can live with that. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:7 | He smiles. | He smiles. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:8 | **MC:** Where’s she working now? | **MC:** Saan siya nagtatrabaho ngayon? |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:9 | **Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts. | **Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:10 | **Rowan:** Then she comes home and somehow still has energy to ask about my day. | **Rowan:** Tapos pag-uwi niya, may energy pa rin siyang mangumusta. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:11 | **MC:** Do you help her out? | **MC:** Tinutulungan mo siya? |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:12 | **Rowan:** I try. Usually she tells me to finish things here first. | **Rowan:** Sinusubukan ko. Kadalasan pinapatapos niya muna ’yung ginagawa ko rito. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:13 | He looks down at his hands. | He looks down at his hands. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:14 — Expression: playful | **Rowan:** Very unfair. All this strength, and she carries the groceries in before I reach the door. | **Rowan:** Unfair nga. Ang lakas-lakas ko, pero naipasok na niya ’yung groceries bago pa ako makarating sa pinto. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:15 | **MC:** Maybe she’s faster than you. | **MC:** Baka mas mabilis lang siya sa ’yo. |
| aTopic12_mayumi_start:16 | **Rowan:** She is. Please don’t tell her I admitted it. | **Rowan:** Oo. Huwag mong sabihin na inamin ko. |

## What happened [aTopic14_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

| Line / direction | English | Taglish |
|---|---|---|
| aTopic14_lola_yes:0 | **MC:** Yes. Please. | **MC:** Yes. Please. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:1 — Expression: sad | Rowan looks down at his hands. | Rowan looks down at his hands. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:2 | **Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down. | **Rowan:** Mas mabilis na siyang mapagod. Minsan kailangan niyang huminto at umupo kahit hindi pa tapos ’yung ginagawa niya. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:3 | **Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city. | **Rowan:** Nag-aalala ’yung doctor sa puso niya. Gusto niyang magpatingin si Lola sa specialist sa city. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:4 | **MC:** Did she go? | **MC:** Pumunta siya? |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:5 | **Rowan:** No. | **Rowan:** No. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:6 | I wait for him to continue. | I wait for him to continue. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:7 | **Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait. | **Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:8 | **Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too. | **Rowan:** Sasamahan sana siya ni Mama. Ako rin. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:9 | **MC:** What about my parents? | **MC:** Paano ’yung parents ko? |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:10 — Expression: concerned | **Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip. | **Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:11 | **MC:** You heard her? | **MC:** You heard her? |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:12 | **Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side. | **Rowan:** Nando’n ako noong tumawag siya. Pero side niya lang ’yung narinig ko. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:13 | He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers. | He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:14 | **Rowan:** Afterward, she said she didn’t want to ask again. | **Rowan:** After no’n, ayaw na raw niyang magtanong ulit. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:15 | **MC:** What did they say to her? | **MC:** Anong sinabi nila sa kanya? |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:16 | **Rowan:** I don’t know. | **Rowan:** I don’t know. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:17 | **MC:** They told me she was fine. | **MC:** Sabi nila okay lang siya. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:18 | He looks at me. | He looks at me. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:19 | **Rowan:** You never heard about the specialist? | **Rowan:** Hindi mo alam ’yung tungkol sa specialist? |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:20 | I shake my head. | I shake my head. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:21 | **Rowan:** I thought you knew she was ill. Just… not how bad it was getting. | **Rowan:** Akala ko alam mong may sakit siya. Hindi mo lang alam… kung gaano na kalala. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:22 | I can’t think of anything to say. | I can’t think of anything to say. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:23 | **MC:** I knew none of this. | **MC:** Wala akong alam sa lahat ng ’to. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:24 — Expression: sad | **Rowan:** I’m sorry. | **Rowan:** I’m sorry. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:25 | He shifts, then stops. | He shifts, then stops. |
| aTopic14_lola_yes:26 | **Rowan:** Do you want me to keep going? | **Rowan:** Itutuloy ko ba? |

## Appendix — additional story text

### Rowan’s letter

| # | English | Taglish |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Hey, {name}. | Hey, {name}. |
| 2 | I turned twenty-one last week. | Twenty-one na ako last week. |
| 3 | Lola made pancit. She said the extra serving was for you, then told me to finish it before it went cold. | Nagluto si Lola ng pancit. Para sa ’yo raw ’yung extra serving, pero pinakain din niya sa ’kin bago lumamig. |
| 4 | I did. Sorry. | Inubos ko. Sorry. |
| 5 | I’m still not sure whether these letters are getting to you. Your parents said you’ve been busy with school. | Hindi ko pa rin sure kung nakakarating ’tong letters. Sabi ng parents mo, busy ka sa school. |
| 6 | I hope it’s going okay. | Sana okay naman. |
| 7 | A few updates from here: | Konting updates dito: |
| 8 | I’m taller now. Properly taller. Lola asks me to get things from the top shelf instead of dragging a chair over. | Mas matangkad na ako. Talaga. Ako na pinapakuha ni Lola ng gamit sa top shelf, imbes na kumuha siya ng upuan. |
| 9 | She still tells me to be careful, though. Apparently reaching for a jar is dangerous work. | Pero sinasabihan pa rin niya akong mag-ingat. Dangerous work pala ’yung pagkuha ng garapon. |
| 10 | And my hair’s long. | Mahaba na rin buhok ko. |
| 11 | I meant to get it cut a while ago, but I kept putting it off. Now I can tie it back. | Magpapagupit sana ako, pero na-delay nang na-delay. Natatali ko na ngayon. |
| 12 | Lola says it’s fine as long as I keep it out of the food. | Okay lang daw kay Lola basta hindi mapunta sa pagkain. |
| 13 | You’d probably have something to say about it. | Siguradong may masasabi ka rito. |
| 14 | I’ve been helping her around the house. Mostly little repairs. I fixed a cupboard door last week, and it only took two tries. | Tumutulong ako sa bahay. Small repairs lang. Naayos ko ’yung cupboard last week. Two tries lang. |
| 15 | She made sure to tell your mom about the first try. | Siyempre, ikinuwento niya sa Mama mo ’yung first try. |
| 16 | Some things here haven’t changed much. | May mga bagay ditong hindi masyadong nagbago. |
| 17 | Your seat at the table still wobbles. | Umaalog pa rin ’yung upuan mo sa mesa. |
| 18 | The hill still feels worse going up. | Mas mahirap pa ring paakyat ’yung hill. |
| 19 | I still look over when someone stops outside the gate. | Napapatingin pa rin ako kapag may humihinto sa gate. |
| 20 | I miss you. | Na-miss kita. |
| 21 | I was going to write something less awkward, but that’s what I wanted to say. | May less awkward sana akong isusulat, pero ’yon talaga ’yung gusto kong sabihin. |
| 22 | Sometimes something happens and I think, “I need to tell {name}.” | Minsan may nangyayari, tapos naiisip ko, “Kailangan kong ikuwento kay {name}.” |
| 23 | Then I remember I can’t just go next door and find you. | Tapos maaalala kong wala ka na sa kabilang bahay. |
| 24 | You don’t have to explain everything if you write back. | Hindi mo kailangang i-explain lahat kapag sumulat ka. |
| 25 | Tell me what you had for lunch. Tell me something annoying that happened in class. | Kuwento mo ’yung lunch mo. O kung may nakakainis na nangyari sa class. |
| 26 | One small thing is enough. | Kahit isang maliit na bagay lang. |
| 27 | I’d just like to hear from you. | Gusto ko lang makarinig mula sa ’yo. |
| 28 | Lola says to eat properly and stop staying up so late. | Sabi ni Lola, kumain ka nang maayos at huwag masyadong magpuyat. |
| 29 | That last part might have been for both of us. | Baka para sa ating dalawa ’yung last part. |
| 30 | Take care, okay? | Ingat ka, okay? |
| 31 | Ro | Ro |
| 32 | P.S. The drawing is still a boat. | P.S. Bangka pa rin ’yung drawing. |
